CStrings:
+ " The saved copies are kept, not overwritten."
+ " images forwarded"
+ "), so previews, clips and masks are being encrypted under a key that never leaves it. This document stays readable here, but anyone you send it to will see empty viewers."

+ "Aspect ratio of the generated video"

+ "Crop the result back to the input's frame — scaling up crops, scaling down letterboxes"
+ "Crop the result back to the input's frame, so the rotated corners turn transparent"
+ "Crop the result back to the input's frame, so the shift stays visible inside it"
+ "Crop — the node emits only this region"
+ "Cropped — this node emits only the cropped region"
